HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0108b10bd9e94ba9867f","text":"The exact return of اسم ربه made this ayah the practical closure of the opening command. 87:11's sidestepping sharpened the ص ل و follower branch: prayer is staying behind the guide rather than moving aside.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000879/B001","root_000879/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_04b04a4221ff9239ad43","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:15:4\",\"root_000879\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Namaz, 87:12'deki zorunlu ateş temasının karşısında bilinçli ve düzenli bir yöneliş yolu olur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000879/B003","root_000880/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_18fcd8b8e01304b5f7f9","text":"2. **Prayer is the voluntary counterpart to exposure by fire.** `ص ل و/B003` anchors ritual prayer, with `ص ل و/B002` adding supplication, praise, and mercy. The near echo with `يَصْلَى ٱلنَّارَ` in 87:12, anchored there in `ص ل ي/B003`, places two similar sound-forms on opposite trajectories. In the fixed ayah, prayer’s role is chosen approach and appeal; in 87:12, the other root supplies compelled encounter. I infer that the phonological echo is contrastive. `فَصَلَّىٰ` thereby reads as an alternative mode of “meeting”: ordered worship rather than fire.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_425743f1e0b336f8b195","text":"1. **Inner recall becomes voiced designation and embodied worship.** `ذ ك ر/B004` gives mention on the tongue, `س م و/B005` gives name as raised designation, `ر ب ب/B002` gives stage-by-stage nurture, and `ص ل و/B003` gives prescribed worship. The `فـ` sequence follows inward `سَيَذَّكَّرُ` in 87:10 and purification in 87:14. Mention externalizes recall; the name identifies the nurturer; prayer enacts the response. I infer that each stage enables the next. The ayah reads as a complete conversion from remembered address into bodily practice.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_463b6013153ead8ce122","text":"3. **Prayer as supplication and mercy, not only legal performance.** Lexical evidence: صلو B002 supplication/praise/mercy; رحم B001 mercy in 87:0; سبح B001 worship/praise. Structural cue: the opening mercy returns through prayer's blessing/mercy branch. Assigned role: صلاة channels the mercy named at the beginning. Abductive move: the packet supplies mercy and ṣ-l-w supplication/mercy; I infer that worship joins the divine mercy economy. Reading change: the ayah reads as remembered-name prayer that receives/echoes mercy.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000879/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_55cc6a68384c6334395c","text":"3. **Following the leader rather than sidestepping.** Lexical evidence: ص ل و B006 (following leader in second place), ه د ي B003 in 87:3 (leading front part), ج ن ب B003 in 87:11 (avoidance aside). Structural cue: the righteous sequence contrasts with the avoider. Assigned role: ṣalā can image the follower positioned just behind the guided lead. Abductive move: ritual prayer trains spatial following of guidance. Reading change: \"prays\" also reads as taking one's place behind the lead path.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B003","root_000516/B004","root_000532/B002","root_000745/B005","root_000879/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6b71b1493f47900a4c28","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:15:1\",\"root_000516\",[\"B003\",\"B004\"]],[\"87:15:2\",\"root_000745\",[\"B005\"]],[\"87:15:3\",\"root_000532\",[\"B002\"]],[\"87:15:4\",\"root_000879\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İçte hatırlanan çağrı, Rabbin adının dille söylenmesine ve ardından bedenle yerine getirilen namaza dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B007","root_000745/B008"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_89ddc9791893aa019b03","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:15:1\",\"root_000516\",[\"B007\"]],[\"87:15:2\",\"root_000745\",[\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Rabbin adını anmak, ilahi adı kamusal ve onurlu bir ün olarak yayma boyutu kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9275f5ea8b1c359b1217","text":"2. **Mention raises the Lord's name into honorable repute.** Lexical evidence: ذكر B007 honorable mention/renown; سمو B008 good repute spread; سبح B001 praise/thanks. Structural cue: remembering the name follows successful purification. Assigned role: ذكر/اسم circulate divine praise publicly and honorably. Abductive move: the packet supplies mention and repute; I infer social/audible spread from the root overlap. Reading change: prayer is not mute inwardness; it participates in raising the divine name.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9e195de258e5ab4c4941","text":"87:16-17 made this ayah a hinge: the purified person remembers/prays, but the audience addressed in 87:16 prefers near life. 87:18-19 also made \"ذكر اسم ربه\" echo inherited scripture: prayer is a present enactment of an old written teaching.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c857e0c58bee8a121162","text":"4. **Avoiding the fire-trap by entering prayer.** Lexical evidence: صلو B004 snares/traps, B001 fire/heat, B003 prescribed worship; صلي B003 fire in 87:12. Structural cue: 87:12 and 87:15 use closely related ṣ-l-y/ṣ-l-w fields on opposite sides of the moral divide. Assigned role: prayer is the alternative contact-point: disciplined worship instead of destructive fire-contact. Abductive move: the packet supplies both fire and worship branches; I infer reversal from placement. Reading change: \"then prayed\" becomes escape from the root-field's dangerous branch.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000879/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_cf4150eb8c3b3336c3a5","text":"3. **Exploratory: prayer enters a transmitted succession.** `ص ل و/B006` supplies the racer who follows immediately behind a leader. The later `ٱلصُّحُفِ ٱلْأُولَىٰ` and the named Abraham and Moses in 87:18–19 activate precedence and following, while the fixed `فَصَلَّىٰ` remains anchored in the prayer root. The succession branch assigns prayer a place behind prior bearers of the same remembered pattern. I infer continuity of practice from the deictic `هَٰذَا` and named sheets; no literal race is intended. The act can thus read as joining an inherited course, not inventing an isolated response.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000879/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d48f7d8f54a51a3964f2","text":"87:18–19 produced the genuinely dormant activation: “first sheets” and two prior bearers made `ص ل و/B006`, following immediately behind a leader, available as a succession image for prayer. They also corrected a private reading of the act; mention and prayer sit inside a transmitted pattern.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_db4a7da4268acea16745","text":"1. **The opening command embodied.** Lexical evidence: ذكر B003 recall and B004 mention; سمو B005 name as raised designation; ربب B001 lordship; صلو B003 prescribed worship. Structural cue: this ayah repeats \"اسم رب\" from 87:1 and adds prayer. Assigned role: ذكر activates the name; صلاة embodies the remembrance. Abductive move: the packet supplies repeated phrase and prayer branch; I infer that 87:15 is the human fulfillment of 87:1. Reading change: the purified person is recognized by enacting the surah's opening command.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000879/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ecb75647ebaf24dcb063","text":"2. **Prayer as supplication/mercy after purification.** Lexical evidence: ص ل و B002 (supplication, praise, mercy), ز ك و B002 in 87:14, س ب ح B001 in 87:1 (worship through praise/prayer). Abductive move: purification opens the person to supplicatory relation. Reading change: prayer is not isolated worship; it is purification speaking and bowing.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000879/B002","root_000879/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f645784b16840d755700","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:15:4\",\"root_000879\",[\"B002\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Namaz, yalnız biçimsel bir edim değil, açılıştaki merhameti yankılayan dua, övgü ve merhamet dileğidir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000879/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_fcce325a7b7a9e64b9ea","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:15:4\",\"root_000879\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Öncekini yakından izleyen kişi çağrışımı, namazı İbrahim ve Musa'nın sayfalarıyla aktarılan eski bir uygulama çizgisine katılmak olarak düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_fd63ad48823ccadb626c","text":"1. **Opening command embodied.** Lexical evidence: ذ ك ر B004/B007 (mention and honorable renown), س م و B005 (name as designation), ر ب ب B001/B002 (lordship and nurturing), ص ل و B003 (prescribed worship).
